  # Investigation Report
  
  ## 1. `packages/server/src/gateway.js` (the `Gateway` class, 5853 lines)
  
  ### Route dispatch
  
  Two transports converge on one method router, `Gateway.routeRequest(connection, frame)` (line **4554**), a big `switch (frame.method)`.
  
  **HTTP** — Node `http.createServer` handler at line **2458**:
  - `POST /v1/rpc/<method>` matched by regex `^\/v1\/rpc\/([^/]+)$` (line 2461) → `handleHttpRpc(req, res, decodeURIComponent(rpcMatch[1]))` (line 2482). The path segment becomes `forcedMethod`; the JSON body *is* the params (line 3648: `params: forcedMethod && body.method === undefined ? body : body.params`).
  - `POST /rpc` (line 2487) → `handleHttpRpc(req, res)` with no forced method; method taken from `body.method`.
  - Also: `GET /health`, `GET /metrics` (Prometheus), `GET /workflows`, `POST /webhooks/<key>` → `handleWebhook`, `POST /v1/electric/write` → `handleElectricWrite`, UI routes via `handleUiHttp`.
  - `handleHttpRpc` (line **3580**) authenticates, builds a frame, then calls `executeRpc(context, frame, cb)` where the callback does the scope gate then `routeRequest`.
  
  **WebSocket** — `ws` `WebSocketServer`; per-message handler at line **3143**. Must `connect` first (line 3149 rejects other methods when `!connection.authenticated`); `connect` → `handleConnect` (line 3251). Otherwise scope-gated then `routeRequest` (line 3158). Frames parsed by `parseGatewayRequestFrame` (line 722); wire envelopes are `{type:"req",id,method,params}` and responses `{type:"res",id,ok,apiVersion,payload|error}` (`responseOk`/`responseError`, lines 652/662).
  
  `executeRpc` (line **2078**) wraps every call in an Effect for metrics/logging; `rpcSuccessEffect` (line 2137) emits extra metrics for approval/signal/cron methods.
  
  ### Auth modes and scope checks
  
  `authenticateRequest(req, token)` (line **3345**). `this.auth` undefined ⇒ open (`role:"operator", scopes:["*"]`). All modes first pass an Origin allow-list (`isOriginAllowed`, line 3357). Three modes:
  - **token** (line 3364): bearer looked up in `this.auth.tokens[token]`; checks `revokedAtMs`/`expiresAtMs`; returns grant's `role/scopes/userId/tokenId`.
  - **jwt** (line 3408): `verifyJwtToken` (line 965) — HS256 only, `timingSafeEqual` on signature, checks `iss/aud/exp/nbf` with `clockSkewSeconds`. Scopes from `scopesClaim` (default `scope`), role from `roleClaim` (default `role`), user from `userClaim` (default `sub`).
  - **trusted-proxy** (line 3440): reads `trustedHeaders` (defaults `x-user-id`, `x-user-scopes`, `x-user-role`); tokenId from `x-smithers-token-id`.
  
  Bearer extraction: `bearerTokenFromHeaders` (line 629) accepts `x-smithers-key` or `Authorization: Bearer …`.
  
  **Scope enforcement** happens at dispatch, not per-handler: WS line **3155** and HTTP line **3651**, both `if (!hasScope(context.scopes, method, this.extensions)) return responseForbidden(...)`. `hasScope` (line 932) → `requiredScopeForMethod` (line 904) → `hasGatewayScope` (from `@smithers-orchestrator/gateway/auth/scopes`). `requiredScopeForMethod` special-cases the raw scope-named methods, maps any `config.*` method to `run:admin`, consults the extension registry for `ext.*`, else calls `getRequiredScopeForGatewayMethod` (defaulting to `run:read`). Extension dispatch re-checks scope defensively (line 5543). Approval RPCs additionally enforce per-approval `allowedUsers`/`allowedScopes` (lines 5256–5262).
  
  ### Event tail (seq semantics + two feed paths)
  
  Per-run in-memory ring buffer: `runEventWindows: Map<runId,{nextSeq, window[]}>`.
  - `getRunEventWindow` (line 1790), `appendRunEventWindow` (line 1804) increments `nextSeq` and pushes a `{type:"RunEvent", runId, event, payload, seq, stateVersion}` frame, trimming to `eventWindowSize` (default `GATEWAY_EVENT_WINDOW_DEFAULT` = 10_000). `getRunEventCurrentSeq` (line 1830) returns `nextSeq` (0 if none). **seq is a monotonic per-run in-process counter**, distinct from the DB `_smithers_events.seq`.
  - `streamRunEvents` RPC (line **4816**, WS-only): validates `afterSeq` (non-negative int, must be ≤ currentSeq else `SeqOutOfRange`), registers subscriber, then in a microtask replays window entries with `seq > afterSeq`; if `afterSeq` is older than the window's first seq it sends a **GapResync** with a full `buildRunSnapshot` (line 1997). Registers a 1s heartbeat (`RUN_EVENT_HEARTBEAT_MS`, `run.heartbeat` events) and a bounded outbound queue (`registerRunEventSubscriber`, line 1839).
  
  **Out-of-process event bridge** — this is the polling tail against the DB `listEventHistory`:
  - Controlled by `outOfProcessEventBridge` (default true) and `outOfProcessEventBridgePollMs` (default `DEFAULT_OUT_OF_PROCESS_EVENT_BRIDGE_POLL_MS` = **1000 ms**). Started via `startOutOfProcessEventBridge` (line 2656), a `setTimeout` loop (`.unref()`); stopped by `stopOutOfProcessEventBridge` (line 2681).
  - `pollOutOfProcessRunEvents` (line **2690**) iterates distinct adapters, `adapter.listRuns(1000)`, and for non-drained runs calls `feedOutOfProcessRunEvents` (line **2724**). That pages `adapter.listEventHistory(runId, {afterSeq, limit: OUT_OF_PROCESS_EVENT_BRIDGE_PAGE_LIMIT=500})`, tracks the max `seq` per run in `outOfProcessEventBridgeLastFedSeq`, JSON-parses `row.payloadJson`/`row.payload_json` and replays through `handleSmithersEvent`. Runs owned in-process (`runRegistry.has(runId)`) are skipped/drained; terminal runs get fully drained then marked in `outOfProcessEventBridgeDrainedRuns`. This is how a gateway surfaces events from runs executing in another process.
  
  `handleSmithersEvent` (line **4301**) is the fan-out: a `switch(event.type)` (lines 4319–4548, ~30 Node*/Approval*/Agent*/Run* cases) that appends to the window and pushes to subscribers.

  ## 2. `packages/gateway/src/rpc/*` — RPC catalog and scopes
  
  Single file `packages/gateway/src/rpc/index.ts`. `SMITHERS_API_VERSION = "v1"`, `GATEWAY_EVENT_WINDOW_DEFAULT = 10_000`. Catalog is `GATEWAY_RPC_DEFINITIONS` (28 stable methods) with legacy aliases in `GATEWAY_RPC_LEGACY_METHOD_ALIASES`. Scope lookup: `getRequiredScopeForGatewayMethod(method)` (line 1160) — handles non-catalog helpers (`health`, `approvals.list`, `workflows.list`, `runs.diff`, `frames.*`, `attempts.*` → `run:read`; `getDevToolsSnapshot` → `observability:read`; `runs.rerun` → `run:write`; `approve` → `approval:submit`) then falls to the definition's `requiredScope`.

  | cronRun | cron:write |
  | listAccounts | account:read |
  | listMemoryFacts | memory:read |
  | listPrompts | prompt:read |
  | listScores | score:read |
  | listTickets | ticket:read |
  | createTicket | ticket:write |
  | updateTicket | ticket:write |
  | deleteTicket | ticket:write |
  
  **Scopes** defined in `packages/gateway/src/auth/scopes.ts`: `GATEWAY_SCOPE_VALUES` (14 scopes, listed in section 1). Hierarchy via `gatewayScopeImplies`: `run:read<run:write<run:admin`, `cron:read<cron:write`, `ticket:read<ticket:write`. `hasGatewayScope(granted, required, methodName?)`: `*` grants all; name-grants (`getRun`) and prefix-grants (`runs.*`) are allowed only if the method's own scope implies `required` (`methodGrantSatisfiesRequiredScope`); legacy coarse scopes `read/execute/approve/admin` mapped by `legacyAccessImplies`. Helpers: `isGatewayScope`, `getGatewayScopeValues`, `GATEWAY_SCOPE_DESCRIPTIONS`.
  
  Other exports in rpc/index.ts: `GATEWAY_RPC_ERRORS` (23 error codes → httpStatus), `canonicalGatewayRpcMethod`, `getGatewayRpcDefinition`, `listGatewayRpcMethods`, `isGatewayRpcMethod`, plus all the `*Request`/`*Response` and `Gateway*Row` TS types.
  
  ## 3. `packages/gateway/openapi.yaml`
  
  OpenAPI **3.1.0**, `info.version: v1`, one server, global `security: [bearerAuth]`. It is a **generated artifact** — `packages/gateway/scripts/generate-openapi.ts` (tested by `tests/generate-openapi.test.ts`) derives it from `GATEWAY_RPC_DEFINITIONS`. 12043 lines.
  
  **Paths:** exactly one `POST /v1/rpc/<method>` per catalog method (28 paths, `operationId` = method). Each operation has `tags: [Gateway RPC]`, per-op `security: bearerAuth: [<scope>]`, and vendor extensions `x-smithers-api-version`, `x-smithers-maturity: stable`, `x-smithers-transport`, `x-smithers-required-scope`. The requestBody schema wraps the RPC as `{id?, method: const <name>, params}` (mirrors the `/rpc` envelope, not the bare-body `/v1/rpc/<method>` form). `components.securitySchemes.bearerAuth` (http bearer, "short-lived Smithers token"); `components.schemas` includes `GatewayScope` (enum of the 14 scopes with descriptions), `GatewayRpcErrorCode` (enum of 23), `JsonValue`, and per-method schemas. No WS streaming paths (streamRunEvents/streamDevTools appear as POST paths with `x-smithers-transport: websocket`).
  
  ## 4. `packages/db` — SmithersDb dialect seam
  
  Source under `packages/db/src/`. Package entry `index.js` re-exports `adapter.js`, `dialect.js`, `sql-message-storage.js`, `ensure.js`, `snapshot.js`, `input.js`, `output.js`, `getSmithersSchemaSignature.js`, `runState.js`, `zodToTable.js`, etc.
  
  **Dialect seam** — `packages/db/src/dialect.js` is the single SQLite⇄Postgres divergence point. Constants `SQLITE`/`POSTGRES`. Exports: `quoteIdentifier(id)`, `translatePlaceholders(dialect, sql)` (`?`→`$1…`, string/comment-aware), `columnType(dialect, sqliteType)` (INTEGER→BIGINT, REAL→DOUBLE PRECISION, BLOB→BYTEA), `translateDdl(dialect, ddl)` (AUTOINCREMENT→BIGSERIAL etc.), `beginTransactionSql(dialect)` (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE` vs `BEGIN`), `jsonExtractText(dialect, columnSql, jsonPath)` (`json_extract` vs `::json->>`).
  
  **Class** `SmithersDb` in `packages/db/src/adapter.js` (line 576), `constructor(db)` (line 591) wraps `getSqlMessageStorage(db)` as `this.internalStorage`. ~90 methods (insertRun/updateRun/getRun/listRuns/claimRunForResume, node/attempt/frame/approval/humanRequest/alert/signal/event/cron/doc/memory/scorer accessors). Each comes in a Promise form and an `*Effect` form.
  
  **Transaction helper:** `withTransactionEffect(writeGroup, operation)` (line 804) is the core; the Promise wrapper is `withTransaction(writeGroup, operation)` (line 896 = `Effect.runPromise(this.withTransactionEffect(...))`). It resolves the sqlite client, serializes via `acquireTransactionTurn()`, runs dialect-appropriate `beginTransactionSql`, and **rejects nested transactions** ("Nested sqlite transactions are not supported"). Transaction bookkeeping state: `transactionDepth`, `transactionOwnerThread`, `transactionTail` (a Promise chain), plus `getSqliteTransactionState`/`resolveSqliteClientKey`/`ownsActiveTransaction`. Reads/writes go through `read(label, op)` / `write(label, op)` wrappers.
  
  **txid / seq helpers today:** there is **no txid helper**. Sequence allocation is per-run event/signal seq, done by:
  - `insertEventWithNextSeq(row)` (line 2186): reads `COALESCE(MAX(seq),-1)+1` under `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (bun:sqlite) or under `acquireTransactionTurn()` for Postgres/pglite, inserts into `_smithers_events (run_id, seq, …)`; PK `(run_id, seq)`. `getLastEventSeq(runId)` (line 2263).
  - `insertSignalWithNextSeq(row)` (line 1912) / `getLastSignalSeq` (line 2004) — same pattern for `_smithers_signals`.
  - Event reads: `listEventHistory(runId, {afterSeq, limit, nodeId?})` (line 2271), `countEventHistory` (line 2279), `listEvents(runId, afterSeq, limit=200)` (line 2287), `listEventsByType` (line 2295) — all delegate to `internalStorage`.
  
  **snake_case vs camelCase:** the DB stores **snake_case columns** (e.g. `run_id`, `payload_json`, `timestamp_ms`, PK `(run_id, seq)` — see DDL in `sql-message-storage.js` ~line 170). The storage layer converts on the boundary: writes go camel→snake via `camelToSnake` (`utils/camelToSnake.js`, used in `buildInsertSql`), and **reads are transformed snake→camel** by `transformRowKeys` → `snakeToCamel` (sql-message-storage.js lines 418/436). So all adapter methods return **camelCase** rows (`seq`, `payloadJson`, `runId`). Booleans re-hydrated via `applyBooleanColumns`.
  
  **Row serializers (DB row → Gateway*Row):** there is **no dedicated serializer module**. The `Gateway*Row` shapes (`GatewayScoreRow`, `GatewayTicketRow`, `GatewayMemoryFact`, `GatewayDocRow`, defined as TS types in `packages/gateway/src/rpc/index.ts`) are produced inline in `gateway.js` helper methods — `listScoresForRun` (line 3974), `listMemoryFactsAcrossWorkflows` (line 3832), `listTicketsAcrossWorkflows`, `listDocsAcrossWorkflows`, `listRunsAcrossWorkflows` (line 3797) — which read the already-camelCased adapter rows and pass/shape them onto the wire. The type comments in rpc/index.ts explicitly note these are the underlying tables "snake→camel cased" (`_smithers_memory_facts`, `_smithers_scorers`, `_smithers_docs`).

(2) The out-of-process event bridge is the only feed for runs executing outside the gateway process (CLI-launched runs) — SSE invalidation latency there is 1000ms+ and the bridge skips in-process runs; tests that assert 1s frames must seed writes through the gateway or they'll flake. (3) Gateway seq (in-memory per-run window counter) vs DB _smithers_events.seq (per-run persisted) vs the new SSE per-connection seq are three different counters; conflating them in GET /v1/api/events?afterSeq= (which must use the DB seq) versus stream frames (connection seq) is the likeliest correctness bug — name them distinctly in code. (4) Row-shape parity: Electric ships raw table rows while several REST rows are computed (computeRunStateFromRow, prompts from disk, approval allow-lists); without the shared-serializer + client-side compute discipline the M3 parity test fails late — freeze the serializer seam in M1. (5) Deleting useSyncQuery/SyncProvider breaks any out-of-repo 0.24.x consumer patterns; ../multi pins old versions and must NOT be touched, but the release notes need explicit migration entries. (6) events collection bound (≤1024 ring) changes useGatewayRunEvents semantics for long runs vs today's 10_000 WS window — pick the bound consciously and document maxEvents. (7) Slow-consumer overflow strategy (invalidate-everything frame) can thundering-herd refetches across many collections on a busy gateway; coalesce refetches per query key with react-query's default dedupe and test the burst path. (8) openapi.yaml is a gated generated artifact and shipped .d.ts drift has burned releases before — regenerate both in-milestone, not at M4. (9) approvals submit must preserve the per-approval allowedUsers/allowedScopes enforcement (gateway.js:5256) in the REST twin or it becomes a privilege bypass. (10) react-query version skew: root already has 5.101.2; adding a different range in gateway-client can produce two QueryClient identities and silently dead invalidations."}

Each change frame: event: change, data: {\"seq\": <monotonic int per gateway process>, \"collections\": [\"runs\",\"run_events\",\"approvals\", ...]}. Frames are coalesced: writes within a 50ms window merge into one frame whose collections[] is the union; seq increments once per emitted frame, not per write. Heartbeat: event: heartbeat, data: {\"seq\": <last emitted seq>} every 15s so clients detect dead connections and learn the current seq. Reconnect: client sends Last-Event-ID (= last seq); the server keeps a bounded ring buffer of the last 256 frames; if the requested seq is inside the buffer it replays the missed frames, otherwise it sends event: reset, data: {\"seq\": <current>} and the client invalidates ALL collection query keys (full refetch). Buffering bound: 256 frames or 64KB, whichever first; overflow evicts oldest and forces reset semantics for stale clients. Client behavior: on change, invalidate the TanStack Query keys for each named collection; QueryCollections refetch. No row-level payloads ever travel over SSE.","txidContract":"Capture helper lives in packages/db as one-export-per-file src/captureTxid.ts (export captureTxid). For the postgres dialect it runs SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text AS txid INSIDE the same transaction as the mutation (BEGIN; <writes>; SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text; COMMIT) and the gateway mutating route returns {ok:true, data:..., txid: string}. For sqlite and pglite dialects mutating routes return {ok:true, data:..., seq: number} where seq is the SSE frame seq assigned to the write's invalidation frame. Client side: SmithersDataClient mutation helpers pass txid to ElectricCollection's awaitTxId in multiplayer mode and pass seq to a waitForSeq helper (resolves when the SSE stream has delivered a frame with seq >= value) in local mode. Node-postgres BIGINT gotcha: txid is returned as text deliberately, never parsed to Number.","collectionCatalog":["runs / RunRow / (r)=>r.run_id / ['smithers','runs'] / no blobs, summary columns only","run_events / RunEventRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.seq}` / ['smithers','run_events',runId] / payload JSON string kept, large stdout truncated server-side to 200KB","nodes / NodeRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','nodes',runId] / output blob EXCLUDED, fetched via node_outputs","node_outputs / NodeOutputRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','node_outputs',runId,nodeId] / blob collection, fetched on demand, never in the base sweep","approvals / ApprovalRow / (r)=>r.approval_id / ['smithers','approvals'] / no blobs","workflows / WorkflowRow / (r)=>r.workflow_id / ['smithers','workflows'] / source text excluded, listed metadata only","crons / CronRow / (r)=>r.cron_id / ['smithers','crons'] / no blobs","docs / DocRow / (r)=>r.doc_id / ['smithers','docs'] / body excluded from list collection","prompts / PromptRow / (r)=>r.prompt_id / ['smithers','prompts'] / no blobs","memory_facts / MemoryFactRow / (r)=>r.fact_id / ['smithers','memory_facts'] / no blobs","scores / ScoreRow / (r)=>r.score_id / ['smithers','scores'] / no blobs","tickets / TicketRow / (r)=>r.ticket_id / ['smithers','tickets'] / no blobs"],"workspaceModeContract":"export type WorkspaceMode = { kind: 'local'; [00:46:44] assistant m1-review-sonnet#1: This confirms the race: `lastCommittedTxid` is a single mutable field on the shared adapter that's set at commit time but only consumed later (in a separate async hop in gateway.js), with no lock held across that gap — a concurrent second write on the same adapter can overwrite it first. That's a genuine correctness bug for the whole point of M1 (txid must identify the write that produced it). This is enough to reject the milestone.
[00:46:56] assistant m1-review-sonnet#1: [tool] ReportFindings: {"findings":[{"file":"packages/db/src/captureTxid.js","line":23,"summary":"txid attribution race: lastCommittedTxid is a single mutable field on the shared adapter, consumed asynchronously after the H…
[00:46:56] assistant m1-review-sonnet#1: [tool] ReportFindings → 3 findings reported.
[00:47:12] assistant m1-review-sonnet#1: {"approved": false, "feedback": "Strong, well-tested implementation overall: real parameterized sqlite+pglite (+gated postgres) coverage for every /v1/api route, correct SSE coalescing/replay-bound/heartbeat/reset design, correct in-transaction txid capture in adapter.js, correct auth/trusted-proxy reuse, and a clean generated openapi.yaml + docs:llms bundle (verified idempotent). However there is a real correctness bug that undermines the milestone's core purpose and one acceptance criterion that isn't actually tested. Must fix before stacking the next milestone on this: (1) captureTxid.js reads/clears `adapter.lastCommittedTxid` in a separate async hop after the transaction's turn is already released, so a concurrent mutating write on the same adapter can overwrite that field before the first request consumes it — the returned txid can be attributed to the wrong write. Needs the txid captured and handed back synchronously within the same turn/lock, not via a shared mutable field read later. (2) The 'slow-consumer test proves bounded buffering' acceptance criterion is not actually met — the committed SSE test only hammers `queueApiInvalidation` directly to check the shared replay-buffer bound, never attaches a subscriber that stops draining its HTTP response to prove the per-connection outbound queue (API_STREAM_OUTBOUND_QUEUE_LIMIT/BYTES + needsReset/reset path) is bounded. Minor: the api/ serializer package (11 files + dispatch switch) is currently pure identity passthrough scaffolding with no behavior — consider deferring it until a real REST/RPC field-shape divergence exists, or drop it.", "issues": [{"severity": "critical", "title": "txid attribution race between concurrent writes on the same adapter", "file": "packages/db/src/captureTxid.js", "description": "adapter.lastCommittedTxid is set inside the transaction but read by captureTxid() only after the HTTP handler resumes post-commit, once the transaction turn has already been released. A second concurrent mutation on the same adapter can acquire the turn, commit, and overwrite lastCommittedTxid before the first request reads it, so the wrong txid (or none) is returned for a given write — the exact bug M1's txid feature exists to prevent, and it isn't caught by the (sequential) test suite."}, {"severity": "major", "title": "no test exercises a genuinely slow SSE consumer", "file": "packages/server/tests/gateway-domain-api.test.ts", "description": "Acceptance requires a 'slow-consumer test' proving bounded buffering.   I have enough evidence now. Let me compile the final report.
  
  ## Review Findings — tsync/m2-collections
  
  ### 1. `/v1/api/stream` SSE route (`packages/server/src/gateway.js`)
  
  Note: grep misbehaved on this 272KB/6536-line file in this shell for unknown reasons (returned nothing even with `-c`), so evidence below comes from `python3` string search on the actual file content.
  
  - Route dispatch: `if ((req.method ?? "GET") === "GET" && url.pathname === "/v1/api/stream") { return this.handleApiStream(req, res); }` (gateway.js, ~L116405).
  - Event shape confirmed: `sendApiStreamFrame(subscriber, frame) { this.enqueueApiStreamText(subscriber, formatSseEvent("change", frame, Number(frame.seq)), ...); }` and `flushApiInvalidation()`: `const frame = { seq: this.apiStreamSeq, collections };` — so yes, `{seq, collections:[...]}` under SSE event name `"change"`.
  - Coalescing: `const API_STREAM_COALESCE_MS = 50;` — `queueApiInvalidation()` debounces via `setTimeout(..., API_STREAM_COALESCE_MS)` before calling `flushApiInvalidation()`. Confirmed within-tick coalescing.
  - Heartbeat: `const API_STREAM_HEARTBEAT_MS = 15_000;` — `subscriber.heartbeat = setInterval(() => this.enqueueApiStreamText(subscriber, formatSseHeartbeat(...)), API_STREAM_HEARTBEAT_MS)`. Confirmed ~15s heartbeat.
  - Outbound bound: `API_STREAM_OUTBOUND_QUEUE_LIMIT = 256`, `API_STREAM_OUTBOUND_BYTES = 64 * 1024`. In `enqueueApiStreamText`, on overflow (`subscriber.queue.length >= API_STREAM_OUTBOUND_QUEUE_LIMIT || subscriber.queueBytes + bytes > API_STREAM_OUTBOUND_BYTES`), the queue is cleared and `subscriber.needsReset = true` is set (rather than disconnecting) — next drain sends a `"reset"` SSE event so the client knows to refetch/resync. Reasonable design, not a silent drop.
  - Cleanup: `req.on("close", cleanup); res.on("close", cleanup);` where `cleanup()` clears the heartbeat interval (`clearInterval(subscriber.heartbeat)`) and removes the subscriber from `this.apiStreamSubscribers` (a `Set`). No obvious leak — interval cleared, listener target (subscriber) removed from the live set on disconnect.
  
  ### 2. Postgres txid capture (`packages/db/src/captureTxid.js`, `packages/db/src/adapter.js`)
  
  - `capturePostgresTransactionTxid()` (captureTxid.js:99-110) runs `SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text AS txid` and validates it's numeric.
  - Confirmed ordering in both write paths of adapter.js (current working-tree state):
    - `write()` (adapter.js L741-775): BEGIN → `writeOperation` → `capturePostgresTransactionTxid(self)` (L753-759) → **then** `COMMIT` (L760-766) → `recordCommittedTxid(self, capturedTxid)` (L767, after commit succeeds). Capture-before-commit is correct.
    - `transaction()` (adapter.js L891-925): operation → `capturePostgresTransactionTxid` (L900-911, gated by `shouldCapturePostgresTxid`) → COMMIT (L912-918) → `recordCommittedTxid(self, capturedTxid)` (L923-924, only if commit succeeded).
  - Call site in gateway.js: mutation routes wrap execution in `runWithTxidCapture(txidCapture, execute)` (gateway.js ~L101275-101279), then after the RPC response resolves, call `await captureTxid(txidCapture, { waitMs: expectsTxid ? 2_000 : 0 })` (~L101791-101795) to retrieve the value recorded by `recordCommittedTxid` via `AsyncLocalStorage`, then `queueApiInvalidation(apiMutationCollections(route.method))`. Design is sound: txid capture happens inside the same DB transaction, before COMMIT, and is only surfaced to the HTTP layer after commit succeeds.
  - Note: `git diff origin/main...HEAD` (committed history only) shows an earlier/different intermediate patch to `transaction()` that stored `self.lastCommittedTxid = capturedTxid` directly (no `recordCommittedTxid` call). That pattern does **not** exist in the current working tree (`grep -n "lastCommittedTxid" packages/db/src/adapter.js` → 0 hits) — it was superseded by uncommitted working-tree edits that route through `recordCommittedTxid` instead. Flag this only because it means the committed history is inconsistent with the working tree; make sure a clean commit captures the final `recordCommittedTxid`-based version, not the stale intermediate one.

Recommend a follow-up targeted check of `listRuns`/`getRun` handler bodies specifically if that's a concern.

  --- FROZEN CONTRACTS (from the Fable design step; treat as hard contracts) ---
  Summary: Frozen architecture contracts for the TanStack DB sync engine: a /v1/api REST surface inside the existing gateway (packages/server/src/gateway.js) mirroring the /v1/rpc catalog with identical auth/scopes, SSE invalidation for local mode (QueryCollection + TanStack Query), and official ElectricCollection over the existing @smithers-orchestrator/electric-proxy for multiplayer. Writes stay on the domain API in both modes; postgres mutating routes return txid captured inside the write transaction. The bespoke gateway-client/src/sync and gateway-react/src/sync stacks are retired in one breaking release while the workflow-UI hook surface (useGatewayRun etc.) is re-implemented over collections so .smithers/ui/*.tsx keeps working.
  REST route catalog: ["GET /v1/api/runs → listRuns → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId → getRun → runs:read","POST /v1/api/runs → launchRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/resume → resumeRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/cancel → cancelRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/rewind → rewindRun → runs:write","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/events → streamRunEvents(tail snapshot) → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/nodes/:nodeId/output → getNodeOutput → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/nodes/:nodeId/diff → getNodeDiff → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/devtools → getDevToolsSnapshot → runs:read","GET /v1/api/approvals → listApprovals → approvals:read","POST /v1/api/approvals/:approvalId → submitApproval → approvals:write","POST /v1/api/signals → submitSignal → runs:write","GET /v1/api/workflows → listWorkflows → workflows:read","GET /v1/api/schema-signature → getSchemaSignature → runs:read","GET /v1/api/crons → cronList → crons:read","GET /v1/api/docs → listDocs → docs:read","GET /v1/api/prompts → listPrompts → docs:read","GET /v1/api/memory-facts → listMemoryFacts → memory:read","GET /v1/api/scores → listScores → scores:read","GET /v1/api/tickets → listTickets → tickets:read","GET /v1/api/stream → (SSE invalidation feed, no rpc twin) → runs:read"]
  SSE protocol: GET /v1/api/stream is text/event-stream. Each change frame: event: change, data: {"seq": <monotonic int per gateway process>, "collections": ["runs","run_events","approvals", ...]}. Frames are coalesced: writes within a 50ms window merge into one frame whose collections[] is the union; seq increments once per emitted frame, not per write. Heartbeat: event: heartbeat, data: {"seq": <last emitted seq>} every 15s so clients detect dead connections and learn the current seq. Reconnect: client sends Last-Event-ID (= last seq); the server keeps a bounded ring buffer of the last 256 frames; if the requested seq is inside the buffer it replays the missed frames, otherwise it sends event: reset, data: {"seq": <current>} and the client invalidates ALL collection query keys (full refetch). Buffering bound: 256 frames or 64KB, whichever first; overflow evicts oldest and forces reset semantics for stale clients. Client behavior: on change, invalidate the TanStack Query keys for each named collection; QueryCollections refetch. No row-level payloads ever travel over SSE.
  txid contract: Capture helper lives in packages/db as one-export-per-file src/captureTxid.ts (export captureTxid). For the postgres dialect it runs SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text AS txid INSIDE the same transaction as the mutation (BEGIN; <writes>; SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text; COMMIT) and the gateway mutating route returns {ok:true, data:..., txid: string}. For sqlite and pglite dialects mutating routes return {ok:true, data:..., seq: number} where seq is the SSE frame seq assigned to the write's invalidation frame. Client side: SmithersDataClient mutation helpers pass txid to ElectricCollection's awaitTxId in multiplayer mode and pass seq to a waitForSeq helper (resolves when the SSE stream has delivered a frame with seq >= value) in local mode. Node-postgres BIGINT gotcha: txid is returned as text deliberately, never parsed to Number.
  Collection catalog: ["runs / RunRow / (r)=>r.run_id / ['smithers','runs'] / no blobs, summary columns only","run_events / RunEventRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.seq}` / ['smithers','run_events',runId] / payload JSON string kept, large stdout truncated server-side to 200KB","nodes / NodeRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','nodes',runId] / output blob EXCLUDED, fetched via node_outputs","node_outputs / NodeOutputRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','node_outputs',runId,nodeId] / blob collection, fetched on demand, never in the base sweep","approvals / ApprovalRow / (r)=>r.approval_id / ['smithers','approvals'] / no blobs","workflows / WorkflowRow / (r)=>r.workflow_id / ['smithers','workflows'] / source text excluded, listed metadata only","crons / CronRow / (r)=>r.cron_id / ['smithers','crons'] / no blobs","docs / DocRow / (r)=>r.doc_id / ['smithers','docs'] / body excluded from list collection","prompts / PromptRow / (r)=>r.prompt_id / ['smithers','prompts'] / no blobs","memory_facts / MemoryFactRow / (r)=>r.fact_id / ['smithers','memory_facts'] / no blobs","scores / ScoreRow / (r)=>r.score_id / ['smithers','scores'] / no blobs","tickets / TicketRow / (r)=>r.ticket_id / ['smithers','tickets'] / no blobs"]
  WorkspaceMode contract: export type WorkspaceMode = { kind: 'local'; baseUrl: string; token?: string } | { kind: 'multiplayer'; baseUrl: string; electricProxyUrl: string; token?: string }. packages/gateway-client exports createSmithersDataClient(opts: { mode: WorkspaceMode; fetch?: typeof fetch; EventSource?: typeof EventSource }): SmithersDataClient where SmithersDataClient = { mode: WorkspaceMode; api: SmithersApi (typed wrappers over every /v1/api route, mutations returning {txid?} | {seq?}); stream: { subscribe(handler): () => void; waitForSeq(seq: number): Promise<void> }; close(): void }. packages/gateway-react exports createSmithersCollections(client: SmithersDataClient, queryClient: QueryClient): SmithersCollections — in local mode each entry is queryCollectionOptions wired to client.api + queryClient with SSE-driven invalidation; in multiplayer mode each entry is electricCollectionOptions pointing at client.mode.electricProxyUrl shapes from smithersElectricShapeCatalog, with onInsert/onUpdate/onDelete routed through client.api and awaitTxId fed by returned txid. Both modes expose the identical SmithersCollections record keyed by the collectionCatalog keys.
  Hook surface contract: SURVIVE (re-implemented on collections, signatures preserved): useGatewayRun(runId: string), useGatewayRunEvents(runId: string, opts?), useGatewayNodeOutput(runId: string, nodeId: string), useGatewayApprovals(opts?), useGatewayActions() (launch/resume/cancel/rewind/submitApproval/submitSignal now calling client.api, mutation results include txid|seq), useGatewayConnectionStatus() (now reflects SSE/Electric stream health instead of WS), createGatewayReactRoot(el, opts) (opts gains mode: WorkspaceMode; the old transport option is removed). BREAK: useGatewayQuery/useSyncQuery deleted, migrate to useLiveQuery from @tanstack/react-db against SmithersCollections; SyncProvider deleted, migrate to SmithersCollectionsProvider(client, queryClient); any prop that accepted a SyncTransport or createSmithersGatewayTransport instance is removed, migrate to WorkspaceMode config. Each break gets a migration note in docs/ and a changelog entry in the breaking release.
  Row-shape parity contract: Shared serializers live in packages/db/src/rows/ (one export per file, e.g. serializeRunRow.ts, RunRow.ts). SmithersDb read helpers and the /v1/api handlers both emit rows through these serializers so sqlite/pglite/postgres return byte-identical snake_case JSON (array columns stay JSON strings, integers coerced with pg.types.setTypeParser(20, Number) where a column is a JS number). Electric shapes stream raw Postgres rows which are already snake_case; ElectricCollection defs apply the SAME Row types and a thin parseElectricRow per collection that only normalizes pg wire quirks (bigint-as-string, timestamptz string) to match the serializer output. Parity is enforced by a parameterized test that fetches the same seeded row via /v1/api and via an Electric shape (gated) and deep-equals them.
  Retirement list: ["packages/gateway-client/src/sync/createGatewayCollection.ts (and createGatewayCollections)","packages/gateway-client/src/sync/createSmithersGatewayTransport.ts","packages/gateway-client/src/sync/SyncTransport.ts (WS sync path)","packages/gateway-client/src/sync/gatewayCollectionDefs.ts","packages/gateway-client/src/sync/createElectricCollection.ts (hand-rolled over @electric-sql/client ShapeStream)","packages/gateway-client/src/sync/electricCollectionDefs.ts","packages/gateway-react/src/sync/createGatewayCollections.ts","packages/gateway-react/src/sync/SyncProvider.tsx","packages/gateway-react/src/sync/useGatewayQuery.ts and useSyncQuery.ts","packages/gateway-react/src/sync/persistence/ (entire unused directory)","all barrel re-exports of the above from gateway-client/index.ts and gateway-react/index.ts","direct @electric-sql/client dependency in gateway-client (replaced by @tanstack/electric-db-collection)"]
  Test matrix contract: Domain-API and provider suites are parameterized with a backends() helper: always [sqlite, pglite (embedded)]; push postgres when SMITHERS_TEST_PG_URL is set; Electric suites run only when SMITHERS_TEST_ELECTRIC=1 (reusing deploy/electric docker-compose with wal_level=logical). Gated variants skip LOUDLY via named test.skipIf with a message naming the missing env var, never red, because CI has no docker/browsers/agent CLIs. Each /v1/api route gets: parity test vs its rpc twin per backend, auth/scope rejection test, and for mutations a txid (postgres) or seq (sqlite/pglite) assertion. SSE suite: coalescing window, heartbeat, Last-Event-ID replay, and reset-on-overflow, per backend. Provider e2e: seed a real gateway, drive QueryCollection invalidation round-trip (local) and ElectricCollection awaitTxId round-trip (gated). No mocks anywhere; fetch/EventSource injected via DI seams, never bun mock.module().
  Phase notes: ["M1 (REST + SSE + txid) must land the packages/db serializers and captureTxid BEFORE gateway routes, so /v1/api handlers never grow bespoke row shaping; update packages/gateway/openapi.yaml in the same commit (gated release artifact) and run pnpm docs:llms.","M1 gotcha: SSE seq assignment and the write's invalidation frame must be produced inside the same code path that commits the write, or {seq} responses can reference a frame that never emits.","M2 (client + collections) adds @tanstack/query-db-collection, @tanstack/electric-db-collection, @tanstack/react-query only now; pnpm-lock and CI workspace topology must be updated together or all jobs red.","M2: re-implement the surviving useGateway* hooks BEFORE deleting the old sync directories, then run pnpm generate:init-pack because .smithers/ui/*.tsx consume the hook surface; verify every seeded workflow UI still renders against a real gateway.","M3 (Electric) only extends smithersElectricShapeCatalog if a collection lacks a shape; do not rebuild the proxy; keep the parity deep-equal test gated on SMITHERS_TEST_ELECTRIC=1.","Every milestone: worktree needs pnpm install first; commit is a later deterministic step, do not commit; jj colocated repo, explicit pathspecs only."]
  Risks: Concrete regressions to guard: (1) .smithers/ui workflow UIs silently breaking because hook re-implementations subtly change loading/error state timing; add a real-browser e2e over the seeded pack. (2) SSE frame loss on reconnect causing stale local UI; the Last-Event-ID replay + reset path needs explicit tests including buffer overflow. (6) openapi.yaml drift fails the gated release artifact check. (7) 200KB stdout truncation interacting with run_events payloads corrupting JSON, a known engine failure mode; truncate at the serializer with valid JSON output. (8) Breaking-release coordination: any external consumer of createGatewayCollection breaks; ship migration notes and a single-version cutover, no dual support.

  GOAL: UI code talks ONLY to TanStack DB collections + useLiveQuery. The collection provider swaps by
  workspace mode: local = official QueryCollection over a new /v1/api REST surface + SSE invalidation
  (SQLite/PGlite/Postgres all served identically through SmithersDb); multiplayer = official
  ElectricCollection over Electric shapes through the EXISTING @smithers-orchestrator/electric-proxy.

  matching. BREAKING changes to gateway-client/gateway-react are expected and allowed.
  
  LOCKED DECISIONS (do not relitigate):
  1. TanStack DB is the stable frontend layer; local mode NEVER pretends to be Electric; no
     database-agnostic sync engine gets invented.
  2. The REST domain API lives in the EXISTING gateway HTTP server (packages/server/src/gateway.js)
     under /v1/api/* — NOT a new daemon. Reuse its auth (token/jwt/trusted-proxy), scopes, and the same
     internal functions the /v1/rpc methods call. /v1/rpc and the WS remain during the transition.
  3. Local reactivity = SSE invalidation (GET /v1/api/stream emitting {seq, collections[]}) → client
     invalidates TanStack Query keys → QueryCollection refetches. NO row-level local sync.
  4. Multiplayer reads = @tanstack/electric-db-collection through electric-proxy shapes (scoping/auth
     live in the proxy). The hand-rolled createElectricCollection is retired.
  5. Writes NEVER flow through shapes. Postgres mutating routes return txid (captured with
     pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text INSIDE the write transaction); sqlite/pglite return {seq}.
  6. Retire in one breaking release: createGatewayCollection(s), the SyncTransport WS sync path,
     electricCollectionDefs + hand-rolled electric, gateway-react/src/sync/persistence/. PRESERVE the
     workflow-UI hook surface (useGatewayRun, useGatewayRunEvents, useGatewayNodeOutput,
     useGatewayApprovals, useGatewayActions, useGatewayConnectionStatus, createGatewayReactRoot)
     re-implemented on top of the new collections — .smithers/ui/*.tsx are the in-repo consumers that
     must keep working.

  --- FROZEN CONTRACTS (from the Fable design step; treat as hard contracts) ---
  Summary: Frozen architecture contracts for the TanStack DB sync engine: a /v1/api REST surface inside the existing gateway (packages/server/src/gateway.js) mirroring the /v1/rpc catalog with identical auth/scopes, SSE invalidation for local mode (QueryCollection + TanStack Query), and official ElectricCollection over the existing @smithers-orchestrator/electric-proxy for multiplayer. Writes stay on the domain API in both modes; postgres mutating routes return txid captured inside the write transaction. The bespoke gateway-client/src/sync and gateway-react/src/sync stacks are retired in one breaking release while the workflow-UI hook surface (useGatewayRun etc.) is re-implemented over collections so .smithers/ui/*.tsx keeps working.
  REST route catalog: ["GET /v1/api/runs → listRuns → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId → getRun → runs:read","POST /v1/api/runs → launchRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/resume → resumeRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/cancel → cancelRun → runs:write","POST /v1/api/runs/:runId/rewind → rewindRun → runs:write","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/events → streamRunEvents(tail snapshot) → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/nodes/:nodeId/output → getNodeOutput → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/nodes/:nodeId/diff → getNodeDiff → runs:read","GET /v1/api/runs/:runId/devtools → getDevToolsSnapshot → runs:read","GET /v1/api/approvals → listApprovals → approvals:read","POST /v1/api/approvals/:approvalId → submitApproval → approvals:write","POST /v1/api/signals → submitSignal → runs:write","GET /v1/api/workflows → listWorkflows → workflows:read","GET /v1/api/schema-signature → getSchemaSignature → runs:read","GET /v1/api/crons → cronList → crons:read","GET /v1/api/docs → listDocs → docs:read","GET /v1/api/prompts → listPrompts → docs:read","GET /v1/api/memory-facts → listMemoryFacts → memory:read","GET /v1/api/scores → listScores → scores:read","GET /v1/api/tickets → listTickets → tickets:read","GET /v1/api/stream → (SSE invalidation feed, no rpc twin) → runs:read"]
  SSE protocol: GET /v1/api/stream is text/event-stream. Each change frame: event: change, data: {"seq": <monotonic int per gateway process>, "collections": ["runs","run_events","approvals", ...]}. Frames are coalesced: writes within a 50ms window merge into one frame whose collections[] is the union; seq increments once per emitted frame, not per write. Heartbeat: event: heartbeat, data: {"seq": <last emitted seq>} every 15s so clients detect dead connections and learn the current seq. Reconnect: client sends Last-Event-ID (= last seq); the server keeps a bounded ring buffer of the last 256 frames; if the requested seq is inside the buffer it replays the missed frames, otherwise it sends event: reset, data: {"seq": <current>} and the client invalidates ALL collection query keys (full refetch). Buffering bound: 256 frames or 64KB, whichever first; overflow evicts oldest and forces reset semantics for stale clients. Client behavior: on change, invalidate the TanStack Query keys for each named collection; QueryCollections refetch. No row-level payloads ever travel over SSE.
  txid contract: Capture helper lives in packages/db as one-export-per-file src/captureTxid.ts (export captureTxid). For the postgres dialect it runs SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text AS txid INSIDE the same transaction as the mutation (BEGIN; <writes>; SELECT pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text; COMMIT) and the gateway mutating route returns {ok:true, data:..., txid: string}. For sqlite and pglite dialects mutating routes return {ok:true, data:..., seq: number} where seq is the SSE frame seq assigned to the write's invalidation frame. Client side: SmithersDataClient mutation helpers pass txid to ElectricCollection's awaitTxId in multiplayer mode and pass seq to a waitForSeq helper (resolves when the SSE stream has delivered a frame with seq >= value) in local mode. Node-postgres BIGINT gotcha: txid is returned as text deliberately, never parsed to Number.
  Collection catalog: ["runs / RunRow / (r)=>r.run_id / ['smithers','runs'] / no blobs, summary columns only","run_events / RunEventRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.seq}` / ['smithers','run_events',runId] / payload JSON string kept, large stdout truncated server-side to 200KB","nodes / NodeRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','nodes',runId] / output blob EXCLUDED, fetched via node_outputs","node_outputs / NodeOutputRow / (r)=>`${r.run_id}:${r.node_id}` / ['smithers','node_outputs',runId,nodeId] / blob collection, fetched on demand, never in the base sweep","approvals / ApprovalRow / (r)=>r.approval_id / ['smithers','approvals'] / no blobs","workflows / WorkflowRow / (r)=>r.workflow_id / ['smithers','workflows'] / source text excluded, listed metadata only","crons / CronRow / (r)=>r.cron_id / ['smithers','crons'] / no blobs","docs / DocRow / (r)=>r.doc_id / ['smithers','docs'] / body excluded from list collection","prompts / PromptRow / (r)=>r.prompt_id / ['smithers','prompts'] / no blobs","memory_facts / MemoryFactRow / (r)=>r.fact_id / ['smithers','memory_facts'] / no blobs","scores / ScoreRow / (r)=>r.score_id / ['smithers','scores'] / no blobs","tickets / TicketRow / (r)=>r.ticket_id / ['smithers','tickets'] / no blobs"]
  WorkspaceMode contract: export type WorkspaceMode = { kind: 'local'; baseUrl: string; token?: string } | { kind: 'multiplayer'; baseUrl: string; electricProxyUrl: string; token?: string }. packages/gateway-client exports createSmithersDataClient(opts: { mode: WorkspaceMode; fetch?: typeof fetch; EventSource?: typeof EventSource }): SmithersDataClient where SmithersDataClient = { mode: WorkspaceMode; api: SmithersApi (typed wrappers over every /v1/api route, mutations returning {txid?} | {seq?}); stream: { subscribe(handler): () => void; waitForSeq(seq: number): Promise<void> }; close(): void }. packages/gateway-react exports createSmithersCollections(client: SmithersDataClient, queryClient: QueryClient): SmithersCollections — in local mode each entry is queryCollectionOptions wired to client.api + queryClient with SSE-driven invalidation; in multiplayer mode each entry is electricCollectionOptions pointing at client.mode.electricProxyUrl shapes from smithersElectricShapeCatalog, with onInsert/onUpdate/onDelete routed through client.api and awaitTxId fed by returned txid. Both modes expose the identical SmithersCollections record keyed by the collectionCatalog keys.
  Hook surface contract: SURVIVE (re-implemented on collections, signatures preserved): useGatewayRun(runId: string), useGatewayRunEvents(runId: string, opts?), useGatewayNodeOutput(runId: string, nodeId: string), useGatewayApprovals(opts?), useGatewayActions() (launch/resume/cancel/rewind/submitApproval/submitSignal now calling client.api, mutation results include txid|seq), useGatewayConnectionStatus() (now reflects SSE/Electric stream health instead of WS), createGatewayReactRoot(el, opts) (opts gains mode: WorkspaceMode; the old transport option is removed). BREAK: useGatewayQuery/useSyncQuery deleted, migrate to useLiveQuery from @tanstack/react-db against SmithersCollections; SyncProvider deleted, migrate to SmithersCollectionsProvider(client, queryClient); any prop that accepted a SyncTransport or createSmithersGatewayTransport instance is removed, migrate to WorkspaceMode config. Each break gets a migration note in docs/ and a changelog entry in the breaking release.
  Row-shape parity contract: Shared serializers live in packages/db/src/rows/ (one export per file, e.g. serializeRunRow.ts, RunRow.ts).   .smithers/specs/tanstack-collection-provider-split.md (read that too).
  
  GOAL: UI code talks ONLY to TanStack DB collections + useLiveQuery. The collection provider swaps by
  workspace mode: local = official QueryCollection over a new /v1/api REST surface + SSE invalidation
  (SQLite/PGlite/Postgres all served identically through SmithersDb); multiplayer = official
  ElectricCollection over Electric shapes through the EXISTING @smithers-orchestrator/electric-proxy.
  Writes go through the domain API in BOTH modes; postgres writes return txid for Electric transaction
  matching. BREAKING changes to gateway-client/gateway-react are expected and allowed.
  
  LOCKED DECISIONS (do not relitigate):
  1. TanStack DB is the stable frontend layer; local mode NEVER pretends to be Electric; no
     database-agnostic sync engine gets invented.
  2. The REST domain API lives in the EXISTING gateway HTTP server (packages/server/src/gateway.js)
     under /v1/api/* — NOT a new daemon. Reuse its auth (token/jwt/trusted-proxy), scopes, and the same
     internal functions the /v1/rpc methods call. /v1/rpc and the WS remain during the transition.
  3. Local reactivity = SSE invalidation (GET /v1/api/stream emitting {seq, collections[]}) → client
     invalidates TanStack Query keys → QueryCollection refetches. NO row-level local sync.
  4. Multiplayer reads = @tanstack/electric-db-collection through electric-proxy shapes (scoping/auth
     live in the proxy). The hand-rolled createElectricCollection is retired.
  5. Writes NEVER flow through shapes. Postgres mutating routes return txid (captured with
     pg_current_xact_id()::xid::text INSIDE the write transaction); sqlite/pglite return {seq}.
  6. Retire in one breaking release: createGatewayCollection(s), the SyncTransport WS sync path,
     electricCollectionDefs + hand-rolled electric, gateway-react/src/sync/persistence/. PRESERVE the
     workflow-UI hook surface (useGatewayRun, useGatewayRunEvents, useGatewayNodeOutput,
     useGatewayApprovals, useGatewayActions, useGatewayConnectionStatus, createGatewayReactRoot)
     re-implemented on top of the new collections — .smithers/ui/*.tsx are the in-repo consumers that
     must keep working.
